The repository contains two physical copies of the same `InterestRateSwap` implementation: `instruments/rates/interest_rate_swap.py` and `session_zip/instruments/rates/interest_rate_swap.py`. Their SHA-256 hashes match. This is a duplicated snapshot, not evidence of the materially different alternate `irs_engine.py` / `price_irs()` design described by the user. `session_zip.zip` is another packaged snapshot (196 entries), but its inspected members did not reveal `irs_engine.py`, `irs_reader.py`, or a direct `price_irs()` function. FRA and swaption modules also construct/use `VanillaSwap` for their own products; they are not alternate IRS pricers.

### IRS files and alternate implementation status

- **IRS-related implementation files found:** `instruments/rates/interest_rate_swap.py`, `engines/analytic/rates_engines.py`, `instruments/rates/fra.py`, `instruments/rates/swaption.py`, and `frontend/static/instruments/rates/irs.js`.
- **Duplicated/parallel IRS implementation:** one identical copy of the IRS class exists under `session_zip/instruments/rates/interest_rate_swap.py`. The `session_zip/` tree is a parallel repository snapshot, not the active module imported by the main application.
- **`irs_engine.py` exists:** **No**, not anywhere in the accessible worktree or inspected ZIP archives.
- **`irs_reader.py` exists:** **No**, not anywhere in the accessible worktree or inspected ZIP archives.
- **Direct `price_irs()` path in `pricing.py`:** **No.** The current endpoint files are `api/v1/endpoints/pricing.py`, `api/fastapi/routes/pricing.py`, and mirrored copies under `session_zip/`; none defines `price_irs()`. The only `price_irs` text hit is a test name in `temp/test_integration.py`.
- **Multiple IRS implementations:** there are duplicate copies of one implementation, but no second materially different IRS engine/pricer among files accessible here. This does not rule out another copy elsewhere.

### Application and executable actually used

No `Optima` process, nor a matching Python/Uvicorn command line for this app, was running during inspection. Therefore the live process path cannot be confirmed.

The checked-in launch wiring says:

1. Source launch `main.py` starts Uvicorn with `api.app:app`.
2. `api/app.py` imports `api.v1.router.api_v1_router`, mounts it at `/api/v1`, and serves the UI from this worktree's `frontend/static` directory.
3. `Optima.spec` and `build.bat` both build from `main.py`; the configured output is `dist/Optima.exe`.
4. `dist/Optima.exe` is present (119,690,861 bytes; timestamp 2026-03-18 12:12 local time), but there is no evidence it is running or that it was built from the current checkout. Its timestamp predates this check and cannot establish source parity.
5. A separate `api.fastapi.app` also exists, but `main.py` and the PyInstaller spec point to `api.app:app`, not that alternate app.

Accordingly, the configured source application path is this worktree's `main.py` → `api.app:app` → `api.v1.router`; the currently used live executable/application path is **unverified**.

No external folder was opened or searched. To resolve completeness, compare this branch/commit with the other Optima copy/version or identify its exact path and branch/commit, then inspect the executable actually distributed or launched.
